Let's design final program architecture:

Top-level constants/functions:
- `eval_internal(a, n)` compute score.
- `full_conv(a)` FFT length 2n-1.
- `score(a)` = 2n max b.
- `softmax(y, bounds?)` stable.
- `obj_grad_softmax(y, beta, n)` returns f, grad_y. Use global? Better pass n,beta in args? JIT not. Top-level function with global `_n`, `_beta` maybe simplest. But multiple minimize calls concurrently no. Need no closures. Use global dict state `_STATE` with n,beta, maybe cache? Hmm.

Define:
```
_STATE = {'n':0,'beta':1.0}
def _smooth_obj_and_grad(x, mode):
    n = _STATE['n']; beta=_STATE['beta']
    y = x - np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(y); a=e/e.sum()
    b = full_conv(a)
    z = beta*b
    m=z.max()
    ez=np.exp(z-m); s=ez.sum()
    val = (m + np.log(s))/beta
    p=ez/s
    # h via FFT
    L=1 << ((3*n-1).bit_length())
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)  # p real, could use rfft
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    # To use rfft, ifft needs full? Use fft simpler.
    r = np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(p,L)*np.conj(np.fft.fft(a,L)),L).real[:n]
    g_a = 2.0*r
    gy = a*(g_a - np.dot(a,g_a))
    if mode==2: return val, gy else val
```

Detailed local optimizer (custom log-sum-exp Adam on y):
```
def smooth_logsumexp(y, beta, n):
    y = y - max(y)
    a = exp(y); a/=sum(a)
    b = full_conv(a)
    z=beta*b; m=max(z); ez=exp(z-m); p=ez/sum(ez)
    val = (m + log(sum(ez)))/beta
    # grad_y
    h = ifft(fft(p,L)*conj(fft(a,L))).real[:n]
    ga=2*h
    gy = a*(ga - dot(a, ga))
    return val, gy, a, b, p
```

Let's design top-level functions in detail.

1. `full_conv(a)`:
```
def full_conv(a):
    n = a.size
    size = 2*n-1
    fsize = 1 << (size -1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fsize)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, fsize)[:size]
    tiny negative due num: return np.maximum(out,0)
```
Values sum ~ (sum a)^2, if sum1 within roundoff. Use float64.

```
def lbgfs_smooth(a, n, beta_factors, time_budget, rng):
   a = normalize(a)
   y = log(a+1e-12)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
   for bf in beta_factors:
       if time over break
       set_state(n, bf*n)
       maxfun = ...
       res = minimize(soft_fg, y, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=[(-50,50)]*n, options=...)
       if res.success/fun ...
       a_cur = softmax(res.x)
       sc = score(a_cur)
       if sc < best_sc: update
       y = log(a_cur+eps)
       # maybe perturb for next beta
   return best
```

Let's implement exact `score` robust:
```
def internal_score(a):
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 0: return np.inf
    a = a/s
    b = full_conv(a)
    return 2.0*n*float(np.max(b))
```
Use for seeds and final.

```

Implement `soft_fg` carefully with global to reduce overhead:
```
_STATE = {'n':1, 'beta':1.0, 'L':1}
def soft_fg(x):
    st = _STATE
    n=st['n']; beta=st['beta']; L=st['L']
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    x = np.clip(x, -100, 100)
    m = np.max(x)
    ex = np.exp(x-m)
    s = ex.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       a = np.ones(n)/n
    else:
       a=ex/s
    b = full_conv(a)
    z = beta*b
    mz = np.max(z)
    ez = np.exp(z-mz)
    sz = ez.sum()
    if sz < 1e-300: p = np.zeros_like(b); p[int(np.argmax(z))]=1
    else: p=ez/sz
    val = (mz + np.log(sz))/beta
    # gradient
    Pf = np.fft.fft(p, L)
    Af = np.fft.fft(a, L)
    h = np.fft.ifft(Pf * np.conj(Af), L).real[:n]
    ga = 2.0*h
    lam = np.dot(a, ga)
    gy = a*(ga - lam)
    # scale by 2n
    scale = 2.0*n
    val *= scale
    gy *= scale
    return float(val), gy
```

def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+budget_s-30
    # load prev
    prev=None

    except: pass
    # n_list
    n_list=[128,256,512]
    if prev is not None:

        if m not in n_list:
           if 64 <= m <= 2048: n_list.append(m)
        else: ...
    n_list=sorted(set(n_list))
    # generate candidates
    candidates=[]
    for n in n_list:
       seeds=[]
       # add prev resampled
       if prev is not None: seeds.append(prev if prev.size==n else resample(prev,n))
       # central
       seeds.append(central(n)); seeds.append(central(n)[::-1])
       # powers
       for alpha in [0.3,0.4,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6,0.7]: add left_power, right_power with small other
       # random powers 80
       for _ in range(80): add two_power random
       # random poly 120
       # random smooth 40
       # random one-sided with shift eps
       for a in seeds:
           a=normalize(a)
           if a.size!=n: continue
           sc=score(a)
           if finite: candidates.append({'a':a,'n':n,'score':sc,'stage':0})
    # sort candidates by score, maybe limit number per n to avoid too many
    candidates.sort(key=lambda d: d['score'])
    # Remove very poor? Keep top 80, but preserve diversity maybe first 8 per n + best overall.

Main optimization loop:
```
best_a = candidates[0]['a'] if candidates else ones(128)
best_sc = score(best_a) if candidates else 2.0

optim_deadline = deadline - 60
while len(candidates) and time.monotonic() < optim_deadline:
   c = candidates[0]
   if c['stage'] >= len(BETA_FACTORS):
       # create perturbed candidate from it if close to best and time
       if c['score'] < best_sc + 0.02 and rng.random()<0.5:
           a_p = normalize(c['a'] + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(c['a'].size))
           sc = score(a_p)

           # insert at end after sort? append
           candidates.append(newc)
           candidates.sort(key=sort_key_candidate)
           continue
       else:
           candidates.pop(0); continue
   beta = BETA_FACTORS[c['stage']]
   remaining = optim_deadline - time.monotonic()
   # time for this run
   if c['score'] < best_sc + 0.01: t_run = min(60.0, max(10.0, remaining/ (10?))) else t_run = min(30, max(8))
   t_run = min(t_run, remaining - 2)
   if t_run <5: break
   a_new, sc_new, smooth_success = _run_lbgfs(c['a'], c['n'], beta, t_run, rng)

       best_sc=sc_new; best_a=a_new.copy(); print
   # Re-sort after candidate improved
   candidates.sort(key=sort_key_candidate)

Let's detail all helper functions.

`_full_conv(a)`:
```
def _full_conv(a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    size = 2*n-1
    fsize = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fsize)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, fsize)[:size]
    out[out < 0] = 0.0
    return out
```
`_score(a)`:
```
def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300: return np.inf
    a=a/s
    b=_full_conv(a)
    if b.size==0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*np.max(b))
```
`_normalize(a)` maybe returns normalized nonnegative sum1, or uniform if invalid.
```
def _normalize(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        arr=np.ones(arr.size if arr.size>0 else 1)
        s=arr.size
    return arr/s
```
Need if size 0? not.
`_resample(h,n)`:
```
def _resample(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size == n: return _normalize(h)
    if h.size <2: return _normalize(np.zeros(n))
    s=np.sum(h)
    if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    x_old=np.arange(h.size, dtype=float)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0, h.size-1.0, n)
    val=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return _normalize(val)
```
`_central_binomial(n)`:
```
def _central_binomial(n):
    a=np.zeros(n,float); a[0]=1.0
    for k in range(1,n):
        a[k]=a[k-1]*((2*k-1)/(2.0*k))
    return _normalize(a)
```
`_power_seq(n, alpha_l, alpha_r, w_l=1.0, shift=1.0)`:
```
def _power_seq(n, al, ar, wl, shift=1.0):
    i=np.arange(n,float)
    left=(i+shift)**(-al) if al >0 else np.ones(n)
    right=(n-1.0-i+shift)**(-ar) if ar >0 else np.ones(n)
    a = wl*left + (1.0-wl)*right
    return _normalize(a)
```
If al/ar 0, ones; if both ones uniform.
`_poly_sequence(c)` uses `_POLY_STATE`.
```
_POLY_STATE = {'n':128}
def _poly_sequence(c):
    n = _POLY_STATE['n']
    t = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    eps = 2.0/n
    x = np.asarray(c,float)
    loga = x[0] + x[1]*t + x[2]*t*t + x[3]*t*t*t + x[4]*t**4
    loga -= x[5]*np.log(t+eps)
    loga -= x[6]*np.log(1.0-t+eps)
    if len(x)>7:
       loga += x[7]*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*t + x[8] if len(x)>8 else 0.0)
    loga -= np.max(loga)
    a=np.exp(loga)
    return _normalize(a)
def _poly_score(c):
    return _score(_poly_sequence(c))
```
Need if n=1? t=1, 1-t=0, log eps okay. We won't use n=1.

_POLY_BOUNDS = [(-2,2),(-4,4),(-4,4),(-3,3),(-3,3),(0,1.6),(0,1.6),(0,2.5),(0,6.283)]
```
9 bounds. If use len check.

def _random_polish(a, time_budget, rng):
    a=_normalize(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
    scale=0.01
    n=a.size
    t0=time.monotonic()
    stuck=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_budget and scale>1e-5:
       improved=False
       # number iter per scale based on n
       for _ in range(20):
          cand=_normalize(a + scale*rng.standard_normal(n))
          sc=_score(cand)
          if sc < best_sc:
             best=a? Actually update best, a=cand
             improved=True
       if not improved: scale*=0.5; stuck+=1; if stuck>3 break
       else: stuck=0; scale*=0.85
    return best, best_sc
```

```
def _select_candidates(cands, n_list, max_total=32):
    cands.sort(key=_cand_score_only)
    selected=[]
    used=set()
    # top overall
    for c in cands[:16]:
       if id(c) not in used:
          selected.append(c); used.add(id(c))
    # best 4 per n
    for n in n_list:
       count=0
       for c in cands:
          if c['n']==n:
             if id(c) not in used:
                selected.append(c); used.add(id(c)); count+=1
             if count>=4: break
    # if still smaller? keep
    if len(selected) > max_total: selected = selected[:max_total]
    # sort selected by key
    selected.sort(key=_cand_key)
    return selected
```

def lp_direction(f, rng):
   M = max(full_conv(f)) * 1.01
   A = build convolution matrix (2n-1 x n)

Let's design top-level `_lp_direction(a, rng)`:
```
def _lp_direction(a, rng):
    n=a.size
    b=_full_conv(a); M=float(np.max(b))*1.02 + 1e-12
    # build A
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), float)
    sm=np.arange(n)
    for j in range(n):
        A[sm+j, j] = a
    c = -np.log(a + 1e-9) + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
    A_eq=np.ones(n)
    res=opt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=M, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=1.0, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'disp':False})
    if res.success:
        g=np.zeros(n); g[res.x]=... normalize
        return g
    return None
```
But A dense for n=1024 2M floats 16MB; loop building O1M, okay. `linprog` may take seconds. For n=1024, maybe heavy. Limit LP to n<=512 and maybe time.

Run `_lp_explore`:
```
def _lp_explore(a, time_budget, rng):
   best=a; best_sc=_score(a)
   n=a.size
   t0=time.monotonic()
   cur=a.copy()
   while time < budget and n<=512:
       g = _lp_direction(cur, rng)
       if g is None: break
       for t in (0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85,1.0):
           cand=_normalize((1-t)*cur + t*g)
           sc=_score(cand)
           if sc < best_sc: best=...; cur=cand? maybe
       if no improvement: cur=best; if time low break
       if sc < best? update
   return best,best_sc
```

def _add_lp_candidates(cands, end_time, rng):

def _lp_explore(a, budget, rng):
    a=_normalize(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a); n=a.size
    if n>512 or budget<5: return best,best_sc
    t0=time.monotonic()
    cur=a.copy()
    stagnant=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < budget:
       g=_lp_direction(cur, rng)
       if g is None:
          stagnant+=1
          if stagnant>3: break
          cur=best.copy()
          continue
       improved=False
       for t in (0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85,1.0):
           cand=_normalize((1.0-t)*cur + t*g)
           sc=_score(cand)
           if sc < best_sc:
              best=cand.copy(); best_sc=sc; cur=cand.copy(); improved=True
       if not improved:
          stagnant+=1
          if stagnant>4: break
          cur=best.copy()
       else: stagnant=0
    return best,best_sc
```

`_random_polish` code:
```
def _random_polish(a, budget, rng):
    a=_normalize(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a); n=a.size
    scale=0.01
    t0=monotonic(); stuck=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < budget and scale > 1e-5:
       improved=False
       # number per scale depends on n: if n<=256 40 else 20
       trials = 40 if n <=256 else 20
       for _ in range(trials):
           cand=_normalize(a + scale*rng.standard_normal(n))
           sc=_score(cand)
           if sc < best_sc:
               best=cand; best_sc=sc; a=cand; improved=True
       if not improved:
          scale *=0.5; stuck +=1
          if stuck>4: break
       else:
          scale *=0.85; stuck=0
    return best, best_sc
```
Uses `a` updated to last improved candidate, not best? okay. Could use best.

def _pick_evaluated(seq_list):

       out=[float(x) for x in arr]
       try: sc=float(evaluate_sequence(out))
       except Exception: sc=np.inf
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_out=out
    return best_out,best_sc
```

```python
"""
Minimize score = 2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/sum(a)^2 for nonnegative a.

Algorithm:
- Build many diverse normalized sequences (previous best if present, central-binomial
  one-sided flat-prefix sequences, random power/asymmetric models, randomized
  log-polynomial shapes, smooth random shapes, Gaussian-cluster shapes).
- Add global low-dimensional pieces via differential evolution on log-polynomials.
- Add LP-inspired extreme directions compatible with current local peaks.
- Anneal a smooth log-sum-exp objective in softmax parameterization using
  L-BFGS-B; keep multiple restarts and diversity.
- Final local random polish and evaluator-based final selection.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import scipy.optimize as opt

_STATE = {"n": 1, "beta": 1.0, "L": 1}
_ETIME = {}
_POLY_STATE = {"n": 128}

_POLY_BOUNDS = [
    (-2.0, 2.0),     # offset
    (-4.0, 4.0),     # linear
    (-4.0, 4.0),     # quadratic
    (-3.0, 3.0),     # cubic
    (-3.0, 3.0),     # quartic
    (0.0, 1.6),      # left boundary power exponent
    (0.0, 1.6),      # right boundary power exponent
    (0.0, 2.5),      # sine amplitude in log-domain
    (0.0, 6.283185307179586),  # sine phase
]


def _cand_score_key(c):
    return c["score"]


def _cand_key(c):
    return c["score"] + 0.2 * c["stage"]


def _full_conv(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a using FFT."""
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fsize)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, fsize)[:size]
    out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def _score(a):
    """Internal exact score for normalized nonnegative array."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    b = _full_conv(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(b))


def _normalize(a):
    """Clip to nonnegative and normalize sum to 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = arr.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def _resample(h, n):
    """Resample a nonnegative sequence to length n by linear interpolation."""
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if h.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if h.size == n:
        return _normalize(h)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    if h.size < 2:
        total = h.sum()
        if total > 0:
            return np.full(n, h[0] / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x_old = np.arange(h.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(h.size - 1), n)
    val = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return _normalize(val)


def _central_binomial(n):
    """Truncated coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2): exact flat prefix convolution."""
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * ((2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k))
    return _normalize(a)


def _power_seq(n, alpha_l, alpha_r, weight_l, shift=1.0):
    """Mixture of left and right power-law falls."""
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if alpha_l > 0.0:
        left = (i + shift) ** (-alpha_l)
    else:
        left = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if alpha_r > 0.0:
        right = (n - 1.0 - i + shift) ** (-alpha_r)
    else:
        right = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = max(0.0, weight_l) * left + max(0.0, 1.0 - weight_l) * right
    return _normalize(a)


def _poly_sequence(c):
    """Log-polynomial / boundary-power / sine shape, normalized."""
    n = int(_POLY_STATE["n"])
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    eps = max(1e-6, 2.0 / float(n))
    x = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    loga = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size > 0:
        loga += x[0]
    if x.size > 1:
        loga += x[1] * t
    if x.size > 2:
        loga += x[2] * t * t
    if x.size > 3:
        loga += x[3] * t * t * t
    if x.size > 4:
        loga += x[4] * t ** 4
    if x.size > 5:
        loga -= x[5] * np.log(t + eps)
    if x.size > 6:
        loga -= x[6] * np.log(1.0 - t + eps)
    if x.size > 7:
        amp = x[7]
        phase = 0.0
        if x.size > 8:
            phase = x[8]
        loga += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + phase)
    loga = np.where(np.isfinite(loga), loga, 0.0)
    loga -= np.max(loga)
    a = np.exp(loga)
    return _normalize(a)


def _poly_score(c):
    return _score(_poly_sequence(c))


def _run_poly_de(n, max_seconds, rng):
    """Global low-dimensional search over log-polynomial shapes."""
    _POLY_STATE["n"] = int(n)
    # 9-dimensional parameter, fixed conservative evaluation budget.
    popsize = 14
    maxiter = 32
    try:
        res = opt.differential_evolution(
            _poly_score,
            _POLY_BOUNDS,
            maxiter=maxiter,
            popsize=popsize,
            tol=1e-4,
            disp=False,
            polish=False,
            seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)),
        )
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False):
            seq = _poly_sequence(res.x)
            if seq.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(seq)) and seq.sum() > 0:
                return seq
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _set_state(n, beta):
    n = int(n)
    size = max(1, 3 * n - 2)
    L = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    _STATE["n"] = n
    _STATE["beta"] = float(beta)
    _STATE["L"] = L


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    m = np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(x - m)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        e = np.ones_like(e)
        s = float(e.size)
    return e / s


def _soft_fg(x):
    """
    Smooth objective:
      2n / beta * logsumexp(beta * conv(a,a)),
    where a=softmax(x). Returns (value, gradient wrt x).
    """
    st = _STATE
    n = int(st["n"])
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    L = int(st["L"])

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.clip(x, -100.0, 100.0)
    m0 = np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(x - m0)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = e / s

    b = _full_conv(a)
    z = beta * b
    mz = np.max(z)
    ez = np.exp(z - mz)
    sz = ez.sum()
    if sz <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(b)
        p[int(np.argmax(z))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = ez / sz

    val = (mz + np.log(sz)) / beta

    # gradient wrt a: 2 * sum_s p_s (P_s a)_j
    Pf = np.fft.fft(p, L)
    Af = np.fft.fft(a, L)
    h = np.fft.ifft(Pf * np.conj(Af), L).real[:n]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h[h < 0.0] = 0.0
    ga = 2.0 * h

    lam = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gy = a * (ga - lam)

    scale = 2.0 * float(n)
    val = float(val * scale + 1e-12 * float(np.dot(x, x)))
    gy = gy * scale + 2e-12 * x
    return val, gy


def _estimate_eval_time(n, beta):
    key = (int(n), int(round(float(beta))))
    if key in _ETIME:
        return _ETIME[key]
    _set_state(n, beta)
    x = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    _soft_fg(x)
    dt = max(1e-5, time.monotonic() - t0) * 2.25
    _ETIME[key] = dt
    return dt


def _run_lbgfs(a, n, beta_factor, run_seconds, rng):
    """
    Run L-BFGS-B on smooth softmax parameterization.
    Returns (best_exact_a, best_exact_score, next_a, next_score).
    """
    n = int(n)
    beta = float(beta_factor) * float(n)
    a = _normalize(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    next_a = a.copy()
    next_sc = best_sc

    y = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    y = y - np.mean(y)
    y = np.clip(y, -100.0, 100.0)

    try:
        dt = _estimate_eval_time(n, beta)
        maxfun = int(run_seconds / max(dt, 1e-6) * 0.72)
        if maxfun < 250:
            maxfun = 250
        if maxfun > 25000:
            maxfun = 25000

        _set_state(n, beta)
        val0, _ = _soft_fg(y)

        bounds = [(-100.0, 100.0) for _ in range(n)]
        options = {
            "maxiter": maxfun,
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-7,
            "disp": False,
        }

        res = opt.minimize(
            _soft_fg,
            y,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )

        if res is not None:
            rx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if rx.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(rx)):
                val1 = float(getattr(res, "fun", val0))
                a_new = _softmax(rx)
                sc_new = _score(a_new)

                next_a = a_new
                next_sc = sc_new

                if sc_new < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_new
                    best_a = a_new.copy()
            else:
                # Fallback with a small perturbation.
                a_new = _normalize(a + 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                next_a = a_new
                next_sc = _score(next_a)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_a, best_sc, next_a, next_sc


def _random_polish(a, budget, rng):
    """Small-scale random perturbation polish in probability simplex."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    scale = 0.01
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    stuck = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget and scale > 1e-5:
        trials = 40 if n <= 256 else 18
        improved = False
        for _ in range(trials):
            cand = _normalize(a + scale * rng.standard_normal(n))
            sc = _score(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_a = cand
                best_sc = sc
                a = cand
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            stuck += 1
            scale *= 0.5
            if stuck > 4:
                break
        else:
            stuck = 0
            scale *= 0.88

    return best_a, best_sc


def _lp_direction(a, rng):
    """
    Solve an LP for a probability vector g such that f * g <= M,
    where f is current a and M is current peak (with slack).
    Objective encourages moving toward underused coordinates.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0 or n > 512:
        return None
    a = _normalize(a)
    b = _full_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(b)) * 1.04 + 1e-12

    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[idx + j, j] = a

    c = -np.log(a + 1e-9) + 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    A_eq = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None) for _ in range(n)]

    try:
        res = opt.linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64),
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"disp": False},
        )
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False):
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if g.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
                s = g.sum()
                if s > 1e-12:
                    return g / s
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _lp_explore(a, budget, rng):
    """Iterate LP-based compatible directions and random convex updates."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    if n > 512 or budget < 5.0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    cur = a.copy()
    stagnant = 0

    for _ in range(8):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 >= budget:
            break
        g = _lp_direction(cur, rng)
        if g is None:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 3:
                break
            cur = best_a.copy()
            continue

        improved = False
        for t in (0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.85, 1.0):
            cand = _normalize((1.0 - t) * cur + t * g)
            sc = _score(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_a = cand.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                cur = cand.copy()
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 4:
                break
            cur = best_a.copy()
        else:
            stagnant = 0

    return best_a, best_sc


def _add_lp_candidates(cands, end_time, rng):
    """Add a few LP-explored candidates from current best seeds."""
    if not cands:
        return cands
    cands.sort(key=_cand_score_key)
    remaining = max(0.0, end_time - time.monotonic())
    if remaining < 35.0:
        return cands

    top = cands[:6]
    per_time = min(30.0, remaining / max(1, len(top)))
    out = list(cands)

    for c in top:
        if time.monotonic() > end_time - 25.0:
            break
        if c["n"] > 512:
            continue
        tb = min(per_time, max(0.0, end_time - time.monotonic() - 20.0))
        if tb < 12.0:
            break
        a2, sc2 = _lp_explore(c["a"], tb, rng)
        if a2 is None or not np.isfinite(sc2):
            continue
        newa = _normalize(a2)
        newc = {
            "a": newa.copy(),
            "best_a": newa.copy(),
            "n": int(c["n"]),
            "score": float(sc2),
            "stage": 0,
        }
        out.append(newc)
        if sc2 < c["score"]:
            c["score"] = float(sc2)
            c["best_a"] = newa.copy()
            c["a"] = newa.copy()

    if len(out) > 90:
        out.sort(key=_cand_score_key)
        out = out[:90]
    return out


def _generate_candidates(n_list, rng, prev, end_time):
    """Create initial normalized candidates for several lengths."""
    cands = []

    for n in n_list:
        if time.monotonic() > end_time - 350.0:
            break
        n = int(n)
        if n < 16:
            continue

        seeds = []

        if prev is not None:
            seeds.append(_resample(prev, n))

        cb = _central_binomial(n)
        seeds.append(cb)
        seeds.append(cb[::-1])

        for al in (0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.5, 0.55, 0.65, 0.8):
            seeds.append(_power_seq(n, al, 0.0, 1.0))
            seeds.append(_power_seq(n, 0.0, al, 0.0))

        if n <= 256:
            n_rp, n_poly, n_smooth, n_cluster = 80, 120, 30, 20
        elif n <= 512:
            n_rp, n_poly, n_smooth, n_cluster = 50, 80, 18, 10
        else:
            n_rp, n_poly, n_smooth, n_cluster = 30, 45, 10, 5

        i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

        for _ in range(n_rp):
            al = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.85))
            ar = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.75))
            wl = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0))
            seeds.append(_power_seq(n, al, ar, wl))

        _POLY_STATE["n"] = n
        for _ in range(n_poly):
            c = np.array(
                [
                    float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)),
                    float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0)),
                    float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0)),
                    float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0)),
                    float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0)),
                    float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.4)),
                    float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)),
                    float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0)),
                    float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.283185307179586)),
                ],
                dtype=np.float64,
            )
            seeds.append(_poly_sequence(c))

        for _ in range(n_smooth):
            k = int(rng.integers(1, 8))
            y = rng.standard_normal(n + max(1, k) - 1)
            ker = np.ones(max(1, k), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, k))
            ys = np.convolve(y, ker, mode="valid")
            if ys.size != n:
                ys = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, ys.size - 1, n), np.arange(ys.size, dtype=float), ys)
            a = np.exp(0.7 * ys)
            a += 1e-9
            seeds.append(_normalize(a))

        for _ in range(n_cluster):
            m = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
            a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for _ in range(m):
                center = float(rng.uniform(0.0, max(1.0, n - 1.0)))
                sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.22)) * max(1.0, n - 1.0) + 1.0
                a += np.exp(-0.5 * ((i - center) / sigma) ** 2)
            a += 0.01 * np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))
            seeds.append(_normalize(a))

        for a in seeds:
            if time.monotonic() > end_time - 300.0:
                break
            a = _normalize(a)
            if a.size != n:
                continue
            sc = _score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                cands.append(
                    {
                        "a": a.copy(),
                        "best_a": a.copy(),
                        "n": n,
                        "score": float(sc),
                        "stage": 0,
                    }
                )

    return cands


def _select_candidates(cands, n_list, max_total=48):
    """Keep top overall plus per-length representatives."""
    if not cands:
        return []
    cands.sort(key=_cand_score_key)
    selected = []
    used = set()

    for c in cands[:20]:
        cid = id(c)
        if cid not in used:
            selected.append(c)
            used.add(cid)

    for n in n_list:
        count = 0
        for c in cands:
            if c["n"] == int(n):
                cid = id(c)
                if cid not in used:
                    selected.append(c)
                    used.add(cid)
                    count += 1
                if count >= 5:
                    break

    selected.sort(key=_cand_key)
    if len(selected) > max_total:
        selected = selected[:max_total]
    return selected


def _pick_evaluated(seq_list):
    """Choose the best sequence according to the official evaluator."""
    best_out = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    for arr in seq_list:
        try:
            out = [float(x) for x in arr]
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not out:
            continue
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_out = out
    return best_out, float(best_sc) if np.isfinite(best_sc) else np.inf


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best nonnegative coefficient sequence.
    Must return a Python list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total_budget - 30.0
    optim_end = deadline - 120.0
    if optim_end < t0 + 90.0:
        optim_end = deadline

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Previous best, if available.
    prev = None
    try:
        h = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if h.size >= 8 and h.sum() > 0.01:
            prev = _normalize(h)
            print(f"[init] previous best length={prev.size}, score={_score(prev):.6f}")
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Lengths to search.
    n_list = [128, 256, 512, 1024]
    if prev is not None:
        m = int(prev.size)
        if m not in n_list and 48 <= m <= 2048:
            n_list.append(m)
    n_list = sorted(set(n_list))
    print(f"[plan] n_list={n_list}, deadline_in={deadline - t0:.1f}s")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial candidate pool.
    cands = []
    gen_end = min(optim_end - 250.0, t0 + 80.0)
    cands = _generate_candidates(n_list, rng, prev, gen_end)

    if prev is not None and 8 <= prev.size <= 2048:
        sc = _score(prev)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            cands.append(
                {
                    "a": prev.copy(),
                    "best_a": prev.copy(),
                    "n": int(prev.size),
                    "score": float(sc),
                    "stage": 0,
                }
            )

    # Low-dimensional global exploration.
    de_end = min(optim_end - 300.0, t0 + 150.0)
    for n in n_list:
        if time.monotonic() > de_end:
            break
        if n > 512:
            continue
        seq = _run_poly_de(n, 30.0, rng)
        if seq is not None:
            sc = _score(seq)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                cands.append(
                    {
                        "a": seq.copy(),
                        "best_a": seq.copy(),
                        "n": int(n),
                        "score": float(sc),
                        "stage": 0,
                    }
                )
                print(f"[de] n={n} score={sc:.6f}")

    # LP-inspired diversity.
    if cands:
        cands = _add_lp_candidates(cands, min(optim_end - 350.0, t0 + 240.0), rng)

    if not cands:
        seq = _central_binomial(128)
        cands.append(
            {
                "a": seq.copy(),
                "best_a": seq.copy(),
                "n": 128,
                "score": float(_score(seq)),
                "stage": 0,
            }
        )

    cands.sort(key=_cand_score_key)
    best_yes = cands[0]
    best_a = best_yes["best_a"].copy()
    best_sc = float(best_yes["score"])
    print(f"[pool] best_initial n={best_yes['n']} score={best_sc:.6f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main annealed L-BFGS restart loop.
    candidates = _select_candidates(cands, n_list, max_total=48)
    beta_factors = [4.0, 16.0, 64.0, 192.0]

    while len(candidates) > 0 and time.monotonic() < optim_end:
        c = candidates[0]

        if c["stage"] >= len(beta_factors):
            # Create one perturbed restart if this seed is promising.
            if time.monotonic() < optim_end - 90.0 and c["score"] < best_sc + 0.08:
                n = int(c["a"].size)
                scale = 0.035 + 0.05 * float(rng.random())
                ap = _normalize(c["a"] + scale * rng.standard_normal(n))
                dp = _score(ap)
                if np.isfinite(dp):
                    cands.append(
                        {
                            "a": ap.copy(),
                            "best_a": ap.copy(),
                            "n": n,
                            "score": float(dp),
                            "stage": 0,
                        }
                    )
                    cands.sort(key=_cand_score_key)
                    if len(cands) > 120:
                        del cands[120:]
            candidates.pop(0)
            continue

        rem = max(0.0, time.monotonic() and (optim_end - time.monotonic()))
        if rem < 18.0:
            break

        beta = beta_factors[c["stage"]]
        if c["score"] < best_sc + 0.02:
            base = 45.0
        elif c["score"] < best_sc + 0.12:
            base = 28.0
        else:
            base = 16.0

        # Do not let one candidate consume too much of a round.
        base = min(base, max(8.0, rem / max(1, len(candidates) + 3)))
        run_time = min(base, rem - 2.0)
        if run_time < 6.0:
            break

        prev_stage_score = c["score"]
        b_a, b_sc, next_a, next_sc = _run_lbgfs(c["a"], c["n"], beta, run_time, rng)

        c["stage"] += 1

        if np.isfinite(b_sc) and b_sc < c["score"]:
            c["score"] = float(b_sc)
            c["best_a"] = b_a.copy()

        if np.isfinite(next_sc) and next_sc < c["score"] * 1.06:
            c["a"] = _normalize(next_a)
        else:
            n = int(c["a"].size)
            c["a"] = _normalize(c["best_a"] + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n))

        if c["stage"] < len(beta_factors) and rng.random() < 0.30:
            n = int(c["a"].size)
            scale = 0.03 + 0.07 * float(rng.random())
            c["a"] = _normalize(c["a"] + scale * rng.standard_normal(n))

        if c["score"] < best_sc:
            best_a = c["best_a"].copy()
            best_sc = float(c["score"])
            print(f"[opt] stage={c['stage']}, n={c['n']}, score={best_sc:.6f} (BEST)")

        candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
        if len(candidates) > 80:
            del candidates[80:]

        # Periodically merge new LP seed if time remains and not near deadline.
        if time.monotonic() < optim_end - 240.0 and c["stage"] == len(beta_factors):
            new_pool_c = {
                "a": c["a"].copy(),
                "best_a": c["best_a"].copy(),
                "n": c["n"],
                "score": c["score"],
                "stage": len(beta_factors),
            }
            extra = _add_lp_candidates([new_pool_c], min(time.monotonic() + 60.0, optim_end - 120.0), rng)
            if len(extra) > 1:
                for nc in extra[1:]:
                    cands.append(nc)
                cands.sort(key=_cand_score_key)
                if len(cands) > 120:
                    del cands[120:]
                if len(candidates) < 48:
                    extras = _select_candidates(extra[1:], [c["n"]], max_total=8)
                    candidates.extend(extras)
                    candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final high-precision polish on global best.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        polish_end = deadline - 15.0
        for bf in (64.0, 192.0):
            if time.monotonic() > polish_end - 20.0:
                break
            rem = max(0.0, polish_end - time.monotonic() - 10.0)
            if rem < 8.0:
                break
            b_a, b_sc, next_a, next_sc = _run_lbgfs(
                best_a, int(best_a.size), bf, min(35.0, rem), rng
            )
            if np.isfinite(b_sc) and b_sc < best_sc:
                best_a = b_a.copy()
                best_sc = float(b_sc)
                print(f"[finalpolish] beta={bf}, score={best_sc:.6f} (BEST)")

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
            r_a, r_sc = _random_polish(best_a, max(5.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0), rng)
            if np.isfinite(r_sc) and r_sc < best_sc:
                best_a = r_a.copy()
                best_sc = float(r_sc)
                print(f"[randompolish] score={best_sc:.6f} (BEST)")

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64) / 128.0
        best_sc = 2.0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final evaluator-based pick among a small pool.
    eval_pool = [best_a]
    for c in candidates[:8]:
        eval_pool.append(c["best_a"].copy())
    if prev is not None:
        eval_pool.append(prev.copy())

    chosen, final_sc = _pick_evaluated(eval_pool)
    internal_best = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    if chosen is None:
        chosen = internal_best
        try:
            final_sc = float(evaluate_sequence(chosen))
        except Exception:
            final_sc = best_sc

    print(f"[final] best_internal={best_sc:.6f}, evaluator_final={float(final_sc):.6f}, n={len(chosen)}")
    return chosen
```
